Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the modular object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 641. The moduli space has dimension at most 248 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_641(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 641. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^248(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by modular generators. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 728. QED.
